नोट - -बाह्य पदार्थ में पपडी, मूग्नी पत्तियां, मिट्टी कण, घुल गंदगी तथा कोई अन्य बाह्य पदार्थ होगा । लम्बा ई फिगर के एक गिरे से दूसरे मिरे तक छ नुरप्रस्थ

गांठों को छोड़कर अपरिपका छोटो गांट प्रोत्र या प्रांतरिक रूप से नुकसानाम्-., खानी तथा मरंन गांठे नाटो हुई गाठे तथा अन्य प्रकार का नुकसानग्रस । 
गाठे सम्मिलित है । प्रविनिविष्ट : सही अर्थ में यह कोई श्रेगा नही है किन्तु सा ] उबंध उन उत्पाषा के लिए किया गया है ओ नन्य श्रेणियों 
के सर्गः नहीं भाते हैं । इस श्रेणी के अंतर्गत हल्दी की गांठो का केवल "पक्ष देण " के विरुद्ध ही निर्यात किया जाएगा ।

टिप्पण - - 1. बाह्य पदार्थ में पपको, सूखी पत्तियां , मिट्टी फण , धूल , गंदगी तथा बाह्य पदार्थ हांगे । 

2. भूरा तथा सृष्टिपूर्ण गठिो में धन लगी गांठों को छोड़कर अपरिपक्व छोटी फिगर और/ या माम्तरिक रूप से नुकसानग्रस्त मोठे, खाली गठि, की 

हुई गांठे और अन्य प्रकार मी नुकसानग्रस्त गांठे हे । 
3. लम्बाई गाटा को अधिकतम चौड़ाई के चिह से मापी जाएगी । 

गोठ में कोर का रंग हाथ से या मटकर से ताजी तोड़ी गयी गांटों में मापा जाएगा । 
4. अविनिर्दिष्ट : सही अर्थ में यह कोई श्रेणी नहीं है घिन्तु इसम उन उत्पादों के लिए पयंध किया गया है जो अन्य श्रेणियों में नहीं पाते है । 

हल्दी फिगर इस श्रेणी के अंतर्गत पावल "पर्म आदेश " के लिए ही निर्यात किया जाएगा ।

का . पा . 3420 - केरलीय सरकार को , मियांस ( स्वालिटी मियतण और 
निरीक्षक्षण ) अधिनियम , 1963 ( 1063 का .:: ) की धाग द्वारा प्रारत 
शाक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, यह राय है कि भारत में निर्यात व्यापार के 
विकास के लिए ऐसा करना प्रावश्यक है और समीचीन है कि अदरक का 
गिर्यात से कर वासिदी नियंसण भीर, निरीक्षण किया जाए । 


सूचना या आता है कि ऐसा का व्यक्ति मा जनस प्रस्तानी मार 
प कोई भाजप या सुसाप देना पाहता है पर रूप से जिसको जस 
राणपत्र की प्रसिया जिसमें पादेश सम्मिलित था , जनता को उपलब्ध करा 
भाती है, पैसालीस दिन के भीतर नियात निरीक्षण परिषद की मंजिल . 
प्रगनि टायर , 28, राजेन्द्र प्लेग , न दिल्ली 110008 को भेजेगा । 

प्रस्ताप 
( 1 ) अधिसूचित करना कि अदरक निर्यात से पूर्ण पवालिटो नियंत्रण 
और निरीक्षण के प्रधान होंगे , 

( 2 ) इस प्राव के उपाध - 1 में यथाणित अपरषः पा मियांत 
( पचालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1980 में प्राहप अनुसार 
यवालिटी नियंत्रण पौर निरीक्षण के प्रकार को ऐसे क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और 
निरीक्षण के रूप में विनिर्दिष्ट फरगा जो रो धारा को उसके निर्यात । 
पर्ष लागू होगा : - - 

( 3) निम्नलिखित को मान्यता मा --- 
( क ) पायात करने वाले देशों के राष्ट्रीय मानको प्रारमर्यात निरीक्षण 

परिष द्वारा मान्यता प्राप्त पारीय मामलों को , 


" सार मान्द्रीय सरकार ने उस्त प्रमाणन के लिए माय विनिर्दिष्ट प्रस्ताव 
बनाए और उगएँ निर्यात ( अवालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम, 
1964 के नियम 11 के उपनियम ( 2 ) को अपेक्षानसार निर्यात निरीक्षण 
परिष की भषा निया . . 


सः घर, म्द्रीय सरकार उपत उपनियम के अनुसरण में प्रस्ताको 
को उन व्यक्तियों की जानकारी के लिए प्रकाणिस करती हैं , जिनमें उनसे 
शालिग होने की संभावना ।

स्वीकृत होगा । अविनिदिष्ट श्रेणी के मामले में कोई सम् यता निर्धारित नहीं है, तथापि यह क्रेता के साथ हुए संविधा की शर्तों पर निर्भर करेगा । 
3. निति अर्थ में प्रविनिर्दिष्ट श्रेणी का श्रेणी नहीं है, लेकिन अन्य श्रेणियों अन्तर्गत न पाने माले उत्पादो के लिए प्रबंध किया गया है । इस श्रेणी 

के अन्तर्गत अदरक मोघल एक स्थायी प्रादेश पर ही निर्यात किया जा सकता है । 
4. "स्थायी प्रादेश " से अभिप्रेत है कि संविदात्मक प्रदरक का संपूर्ण मूल्य भारत में शतप्रतिशत परिवर्तनीय प्रत्यय पत्र , ऋजिट पन खोलकर पग्रिम रूप 

में प्राप्त किया गया हो जो कि लदान रसीव से समधिन पोट परिवहन पक्ष के प्रस्तुतीकरण पर नकदी करण हो सके अथवा किसी अन्य 
प्रकार से प्रत्याभून हो ।

से कम नहीं 

( 1 ) जिजिंबर आफिणिनेल रोज के सुखाएं हुए गाइजोम टुकड़ों में , 
भाकार एवं प्रकार में अनियमित प्रकार में फाइबर मेश विशेषतामों 
सहित रंग में पीला भूरा छिलका पूरी तरह न उतरा हुमा तपा 

गारवलिंग धारा छोटे टुकड़े हटाये हुए, 
( 2 ) विशिष्टि स्वाद एवं सुगन्ध होगी , साबुत होनी चाहिए तथा बासी 

या तीखा स्वाद न हो प्रथया फफूंदीवार गंध न हो । 
( 3 ) पर्याप्त ( उचित ) रुप में सून्त्री हुए तथा फवी एवं रोगाणु 

उत्पीड़न से मुफ्त होगी । 
टिप्पणी :- प्राकार के लिए महायताएं : अधिकतम 3 प्रतिशत सहयताएं प्रमुभव होंगी । याहय सामग्रो से अशक्त अथवा स्पेण्ट प्रवरक सहित सभी बाहा पदार्ग 
अभिप्रेत है ।

पविकृत प्रविरंजित कोचीम 
( एनयूजीसी ) एन एस प्रविनि 
विष्ट में 


* जैसा कैसा के साथ संविधा में विनिर्दिष्ट होगा । 
टिप्पण : 

1. यह सामग्री के प्रशक्त स्पेकट अथवा प्रदरक सहित सभी बाप पदार्य मभिप्रेत है । 

2. दुर्घटनाग्रस्त बुटियों के कारण यह सहायता का प्राकार "विशेष " श्रेणी के मामले में 7 प्रतिशत अथवा " उत्तम " श्रेणी के मामले में 15 प्रतिशत तक 
स्वीकृत होगा । विनिर्दिष्ट श्रेणी के मामले में कोई साता निर्धारित नहीं है, तथापि यह नेता के साथ हए संविवा की गतों पर निर्भर होगा । 

3. निश्चित अर्थ में प्रविनिविष्ट श्रेणी कोई श्रेणी नहीं है, लेकिन अन्य श्रेणियों के अन्तर्गत न माने वाले उत्पादों के लिए बंध किया गया है । इस श्रेणी 
के अन्तर्गत भवरक केवल एक स्थायी प्रावेश यह ही निर्यात किया जा सकता है । 

4. " स्थायी मादेश " से अभिप्रेत है कि सेविवात्मक अदरक का संपूर्ण मूल्य भारत में शतप्रतिशत अपरिवर्तनीय प्रत्यय पत्र (क्रेडिट पत्र ) खोलकर मग्रिम 
रूप में प्राप्त किया गया हो जो कि लदान रसीद के समर्थित पोत परिवहम पत्र के प्रस्तुतीकरण पर नकवीकरण हो सके मथवा किसी अन्य प्रकार से प्रत्याभूत हो ।

श्रेणा विवरण

टिप्पण : 

1. पाय सामग्री के प्रशस प्रयवा स्पेशट अदरक सहित सभी बाहय पदार्थ अभिप्रेत है । 

2. दुर्घटनाग्रस्त नदियों के कारण से समता का आकर "विशेष " श्रेणी के मामले में 7 प्रतिशत अथवा " उत्तम " श्रेणी के मामले में 15 प्रतिशत तक 
स्वीकृत होगा । विनिर्दिष्ट श्रेणी में मामले में कोई सहायता निर्धारित नहीं है, तथापि यह क्रेता के साथ हुए संविधा को गौ पर निर्भर होगा । 

3. निश्चित अर्धे में अविनिर्दिष्ट श्रेणी नहीं है, लेकिन अन्य श्रेणियों के अन्तर्गत म पाने वाले उत्पादों के लिए प्रवेश किया गया है । इस श्रेणा के 
मन्तर्गत प्रदरक केवल एक स्यागी मावेश पर ही निर्यात किया जा सकता है । 

4. "स्यायी प्रादेश " से अभिप्रेत है फि सविदात्मक अपरक का संपूर्ण मल्य भारत में शतप्रतिशत भपरिवर्तनीय प्रत्यय पत्र ( केष्टि पत्र ) खोनकर प्रग्निम 
रूप में प्राप्त किया गया हो जो कि लदान रसोद से समर्थित पोत परिवहन पन के प्रस्तुतीकरण पर नकदीकरण हो सके मथवा किसी अन्य प्रकार से प्रत्याभस

2. दुर्घटनाग्रस्त त्रुटियों के कारण से सहसा का प्राकार “विशेष " श्रेणी के मामले में 7 प्रतिशत प्रथया " उतम " श्रेणी के मामले में 15 प्रतिशत तक 

स्वीकृत होगा । अविनिर्दिष्ट श्रेणी के मामले में कोई माना निर्धारित नहीं है, तथापि यह क्रेता के साथ खुए संविधा को शो पर निर्भर होगा । 


दुर्घटनाग्रस्त त्रुटियों के कारण से समता का प्राकार “विशेष श्रेणी के मामले मेंया प्रतिमान गाथका "उलाभ घेणी के मामले में 15 प्रतिनाव तक 


3. निश्चित अर्थ में विनिर्दिष्ट श्रेणी कोई श्रेणी नहीं है, लेकिन अन्य श्रेणियों के अंतर्गत न माने वाले उत्पादों के लिए प्रबंध किया गया है । इस 

श्रेणी के अंतर्गत प्रदरक केवल एक स्थायी प्रावेग पर ही निर्यात किया जा सकता है । 


4. "स्थायी प्रादेश " से अभिप्रेत है कि संधिवात्मक अदरक का संपूर्ण मूल्य भारत में सप्रतिशत परिवर्तनीय प्रत्यय पत्र ( क्रेडिट-पन्न ) खोलकर 

मग्रिम रूप में प्राप्त किया गया हो जो कि पोत लवान की रसोद से समर्थित पोम परिवहन पत्र के प्रस्तुतीकरण पर नकदीकरण हो सके अथवा 
किसी अन्य प्रकार से प्रत्याभूत हो ।

S .O . 3420.---Whereas in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sectiou 6 of the Export (Quality Control and Inspection ) Act, 
1963 (22 of 1963), the Central Government is of the opinion 
that it is necesary and expedient so to do for the develop 
ment of the Export Tradio of India that ginger should be suh 
ject to quality control and inspection prior to export ; 


13 ) to recogniso 
(a ) the national standards of importing countries and

Inspection Council ; 


(b ) the contractual specifications agreed to between tho 

foreign blyer and tho exporter provided that such 
sredications are not bclow the minimum specifica 
tion as set out in the Schedule I to LX to this 
Order; 


And whereas , the Central Government has formulated the 
proposals specified below for the said purpose and has for 
warded the same to the Export Inspection Council , as required 
by sub -rule (2 ) of rule 11 of the Export (Quality Control and 
Inspection ) Rules , 1964; 


(c ) tho grade designation formulated under the Ginger

tral Government hereby publishes the said proposals for the

(d ) in the absence of contractual specifications, the 

minijum Specifications get out in the Schedulo - I 
to IX to the Order provided that the specifications 
shall also conform to the Food Laws, if any, in

posuls may forwarded the same within forty -five days on

of the Export Suspection agencies established under section 7

aforesaid standard specificalons and is exportworthy . 


PROPOSALS 


(1 ) to notify that ginger shall be subject to quality con 
trol and inspection prior to export; 


(2 ) to specify the type of quality control and Inspection 
in accordance with the draft Export of Ganger (Quality 
Contiol and Inspection ) Rules, 1990 as set out in the 
Annexure - I to this Order , as the type of quality control and 
inspection which shall be applied to guch ginger prior to 
their export; 


2 . Nothing in this Order shall apply to export by sea , 
land or air of bonafide samples of ginger not exceeding in 
value of rupees fiſty to prospective buyers . 


3 . In this notification " ginger " means (Zinigiber Oncinalo 
whether wholo or powdered , produced in India .

( ii ) for clauso ( d ) , the following shall be substituted , 

ramely : 


" ( xiफ ) केन्द्रीय सरकार पर्यावरण और विस्थापित व्यक्तियों 
के पुनर्वास के क्षेत्र में अनुभव रखने वाले एक ऐसे व्यक्ति को 
जो भारत सरकार के संयुक्त सचिव या राज्य सरकार के अपर 
सचिव की पंक्ति से नीचे का न हो स्वतन्त्र सवस्य नियुक्त 

करेगी - सदस्य 
( ii ) खण्ड ( घ ) के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित रखा जाएगा, अर्थात् : - - 

" अ " जब भारत सरकार के सचिव या राज्य सरकारों के मुख्य 
सचिव प्राधिकरण की मैठकों में उपस्थित होने में असमर्थ हो 
पाएं तो में , अपने प्रतिनिधियों को जो यथास्थिति , भारत 
सरकार के संयुक्त सचिव या राज्य के सचिव से नीचे की पंक्ति 

के न हों भेज समोंगे । " 
3. उक्त स्कीम के पैरा के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित पैरा रखा 

जाएगा , अर्थास् : 
".: गणपूर्ति मौर मतवान -- - प्राधिकरण की मैठक की गणपूर्ति ( नी 
फारवार को छोड़कर ) माठ सदस्यों की होगी इसमें से कम से कम एक -एक 

मवस्य भाग लेने वाले चार राज्यों में से तीन राज्यों में से प्रत्येक 
से होगा । किसी राज्य के हित को प्रभावित करने वाला कोई 
भी विनिश्चय उस राज्य के कम से कम एक राज्य को 
उपस्थिति के बिना नहीं लिया जाएगा । नेमी कारयार के संध्यवहार 
की गणपूति पांच सदस्यों की होगी । प्रत्येक प्रश्न नेमी कारबार 
से भिन्न को प्राधिकरण की किसी मैठक के समक्ष लाया जाता 
है उस बैठक में जिसके समक्ष ऐसा विषय लाया जाता है उपस्थित 
और मतदान करने वाले सदस्यों के बहुमत से विनिश्मित किया 
आएगा । प्राधिकरण किसी भो ऐसे कारबार को जिसमें एक 
राज्य से अधिक के राज्यों के हितों को प्रभावित करने वाले

को मध्यक्षता करेगा । मतों के बराबर होने की दशा में अध्यक्ष

" Whether the action of the management of State Bank 

of India in relation to their Damoh Branch (M . P . ) 
in terminating tho services of Shri Salim Ahmed 
Qureshi, Messenger, w .e . f. 8 - 2 - 85 and not consider 
ing him for further employment under Section 
25 - H of the industrial Dispuids Act is justified ? 

If so , to what relief is the workman entitled ? " 
2 . Undisputed facts of the case are that the workman con 
cerned was employed by the management as Messenger and 
according to the order of reference his servicos were termi 
nated with cffect from 8 - 2 - 85. It is also not disputed that the 
workman was appointed on 10 - 11 - 1984 . 

3 . The case of the workman in brief is that he was appoin 
ted on existing post falling under the category of permanent 
emplovec with effect from 10 - 11 - 1984. His services were 
abruptly stopped from 8 - 2 - 1985 and one Krishna Gopal Sharma 
was appointed in his place which further proves that he was 
appointed against a permanent pont. His removal is against 
the various Agreements and Awards including the Desai Award 
111d Bipartite Settirments estered into between the Bank and 
the representative of the Union . Managoment has therefore 
violated the provisions of Sec . 25 - G & H of the I.D . Act. 
He is therefore entitled to be reinstated with all back wages 
and consequential tenefits . 

4 . The case of the management in short is that the work 
man was orally appointed on daily wages and his services 
were orally terminated with eflect from 2 - 2 - 1985 . He was 
not appointed against any rermanent vacancy nor anybody 
else wne spirointec in 1 “ place . He has only worked for 85

7 . Though in the statement filed by the Union the legality 
of the domestic enquiry conducted by the management was 
vigourously called in question , contending that it was con

The dispute between the parties referred to above in con 
nection with the punishments awarded to a workman was 
referred to this Court for adjudication under Section 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) 
of the Industrial Diguts by the Central Government by 
Order No . L - 12012( 11 ) / 88 -0I ( E ) /DJJC ( A ) dated 7th 
February, 1989 . On receipt of this reference order in this 
Court both the managenicnt and the Union entered appea 
rance and filed statements . 

2 . The brief averments in the statement filed by the Union 
are us follows ;- The workman by name Punnoose Kurian , 
WAS working as a Junſor Clerk in the Kannoth Branch of the 
management bank . Whilo so a chargestreet was issued to him 
by the management setting up two charges against him . Tho 
first charge is that on 16 - 2 - 1984 he borrowed and misappro 
priated a sum of Rs. 300 from the daily collection made by 
the N . N . D . Agent. The second charge is that the workman 
issued an undated challan counter- foil for Rs. 300 uffixing 
cash receipt seal to one E . D . Thomas who was a lounce of 
the bank without accounting that amount. In fact the work 
man is innocent. Hence he gave n reply to the chargesheet 
pleading innocence . But however the management was not 
satisfied with his explanation and a domestic enquily was 
conducted in this matter by the management and one Sen or 
Manager of the Bank by name K . A . George was appointed as 
the Enquiry Officer. The Enquiry Ocer conducted this 
enquiry violating all principles of natural justice. In fact the 
sa d enquiry was a farco . In that enquiry the workmon was 
not given sufficient opportunity to state his casc and defend 
himself. But however later the Enquiry Officer gavo a report 
to the management finding the workman guilty of both the 
charges and hasing on that report of the Enquiry Officer the 
management bank stopped the promotion of the workman 
till 1991 as punislıment for the second charge and as penalty 
for the first charge his increment in the time scale of pay 
was degraded hy one state . This act on of the management 
is illegal and unjust as the workman is absolutely innoccnt . 
Moreover the finding of the Enquiry Officer holding the 
workman guilty is really perverse as there is absolutely nu 
evidenco in that enquiry 19 Sulytain either of these two charges . 
Hence in any event basing on that report, the manageincnt 
should not have punished the workman . Hence an award 
may be passed setting aside these two punishments imposed 
by the management. 

3 . The brief averments in the statement filed by the 
management are as follows : - - It is true that this workman 
was working as a Junior Clerk in the Kannuth Branch of this 
bank . It is also true that a chargo slicct was served to him 
setting up the two charges onumeratecl in the statement filed 
by the Union . But the averments in the statement filed by 
the Union that the workman is not guilty of these two charge , 
and that ho is innocent is absolutely false . The further allege 
tion in the statement filed by the Union that the domcstic 
cnquiry conducted by the management was a farco and that 
it was conducted violating all norms of natural justice is 
absolutely false . In fact the workinon was given all opportu 
nities to defend himself and state his case . The management 
chose to punish this workman only because in that impartial 
enquiry he was found guilcy of these two cliarges. Even 
then the management was very lenient to this workman as the 
punishment imposed are only minor punishments even though 
the charges proved against the workmaji were grave . Hence 
the workman is not entitled to any relief as claimed by the 
Union . Hence an award may be passed upholding these 
actions of the management. 

4 . On these pleadings the following points are formulated 
for decision : 
( 1) Wiether the finding of the Enquiry Officer holding 

the workman quilty is based on legal evidence and 

that it is sustainable in law ? 
(2 ) Whiant 11e the pronet punishment if any to be 

awarded to the workman ? 
( 3 ) Result ?

The Management Company has got a certified Standing 
Ordçis dated 14 - 5 - 1980. According to Clause 12 ( a ) of the 
Sianding Orders, a worker who abycats himself for a continu 
013 period of 8 days or fails to return for work within 
8 day ; of the last day of leave sanctioned to him , shall loose 
liis lien on his job linless he explains to the satisfaction of 
thic Cuimpany that the absence was due to reasons beyond 
his control to irzimute the Company of such absence in time. 
This clause further provides that an enquiry will be held 
Lyy the Company before taking action against the workman . 
Shri V , Sahadevan unauthorisedly absented from duty with 
011 any intimation from work from 20 - 5 - 1982 . A memo of 
Clary was issued to him . This memo issued to his home 

dress furnished to the Company, was returned with an 
endorsement of postal authorities that addressee left present 
address not known . As no explanation was forth coming 
from the workcr , the management arranged an enquiry into 
the matter and the appointinent of the enquiry officer was 
intimated to the workman concerned by a registered notice . 
This notice was also returned undelivered with the endorso 
ment of the postal authorities that addressee left without 
instruction . Thereafter the copy of the noticc was published 
in all Notice Boards of the Company . Pursuant to this tho 
Titanium Workers Union informed the management that 
Sij Sahadevan was a member of their union and was under 
fonu medical treatment consequent on a scooter accident. 
They also produced a medical certificato issued by the Gov 
ernirent Rural Dispensary , Vedi. On the basis of the request 
of the Union , the enquiry was postponed and the union was 
informed to direct Sri Sahadevan to appear before the Com 
fany s Medical Office for a medical check up . The work 
nian did not appear himself before the Medical Officer of 
the Company as directed and the said union did not pursue 
his cauce further. The Management proceeded with the 
enquiry on 24 - 2 -83 and the same was concluded on the very 
same day . The Enguiry Officer submitted a report on 3 - 3 - 83 
holding that the absence of Sri Sahadevan from 20 - 5 - 82 is 
unauthorised and deserves to be proceeded against undor 
Clause 12 of the Standing Orders . Accepting the report, 
the management has removed him from service invoking the 
powers under the said clause of the standing orders . The 
work man has come forward with a petition on 25- 9 - 1983 
levuesting to rermit him to join duty . This was not accept 
able to the management, Subsequently he filed a petition on 
9 - 10- 85 before the Asstt. Labour Conmissioner which has 
culruinated to this reference. The management has complied 
with the principles of natural justice in removing the work 
man from the service of the Company . The action taken 
against the worher jg legal and justified and may not be 
intcfcrcd with .

AWARD 


The industrial dispute between the above parties was refer 
Ted to this Court for adjudication by the Government of 
India , Ministry of Lahour, New Delhi as per Order No . 
L - 2901227 86 - D . III( B ) DII dated 13 - 11 - 1987. The issue 
referred for adjudication in the following :--- 


" Whether the action of the management of Travancorc 

Titanium Products Ltd ., Trivandrum in dismissing 
from service Shri V . Sahadevan , Process Operator, 
is justified ? If not , to what relief is the workman 
entitled " 


3. The points that arise for consideration is whether the 
enquiry conducted by the enquiry officer is lcgul and proper 
adhering to the principles of natural justice and whether the 
findings entered into by the Enquiry Officer are supported 
by legal evidence ? 


II, Disciplinary proceedings were initiated by the Manage 
ment against the workman in which a domestic enquiry 
was conducted . The dismissal followed on the basis of tho 
findings of the Enquiry Officer , I have narrated the facts 
in detail in my preliminary order dated 24- 9 -1990 wherein 
the validity of the domestic enquiry and the findings of tho 
Enquiry Officer have been upheld by me. It is unnecessary 
to revent the facts. Instead I shall reproduce that order in 
full here - 


4 . Tlie Manoyeinert has produced the filo relating to tho 
donie :tic enquiry and the same was marked as Ext. Mi. 


" PRELIMINARY ORDER 


The industrial dispute between the above parties was rcfer 
red to this Court for adjudication by the Government of 
India , Ministry of Labour, New Delhi as per Order No. L 
29012 / 27 /86, -DIT : A ) / D . IV , dated 13 -11- 1987. The following 
is the issue refcired for adjudication - 


5 . The delinquent was a workman in the Management 
Company . While so , he was served with a memo of charges 
for unauthorised absence from duty . The notice issued to 
1lie delinquent was returned unserved . Thereafter an cnquiry 
way ordered to be conducted and an enquiry officer was 
appointed . He conducted the enquiry and made his report 
1inding that the delinquent is guilty of the clierge , The Man 

gement has considered the finding of the enquiry officer and 
came to tlic conclusion that the wuikinan shall be dismissed 
from ervice . This order of the management dismissing the 
delinquent led to the present reference . The workman has 
not filed any claim statement challenging the validity and 


" Whetlier the action of the management of Travancore 

Titanium Products Ltd ., Trivandrum in dismissing

the legality of the enquiry and the finding . Therefore the 
enquiry conducted by the enquiry officer is to be held valid 
as it was held in accordance with the principles of natural 
justice. It can also be seen that the enquiry officer nas entered 
a finding relying on the legal evidence . Therefore I find that 
the enquiry officer has conducted the enquiry properly and 
the finding entered into by the enquiry officer is also legal 
and proper .

III . The question remains to be considered is regarding 
the jusifiability and the propriety of the punishment imposed 
on ve del.nyient, who was dismissed from service for the 
misconduct of unauthoried absence from duty. The delin 
cuert has not file. ! a claim statement in this case challenging 
thie val dity and the legality of the enquiry . According to 
Claire 12 (a ) of the Standing Orders of the Management 
Company , a worker who absents himself for a con inuous 
period of 8 days or fails to return for work within 8 days 
of the last day of leave sanctioned to him , shall loose his 
lien on his job unless he explains to the satisfaction of the 
Company that the absence was due to reasons beyond his 
control to intimate the Company of such absence in time. 
So in view of Clause 12 ( a ) of the Standing Orders the 
punishment imposed on the delinquent by the Management 
is proper and justifiable . Hence I see no reason to interfere 
with the punishment imposed on the workman by invoking 
Sec. 11- A of the I. D . Act.

The Govt . of India , Ministry of Labour in exercise of the

Act, 1947 has referred he following dispute to this Tribunal 
for adjudicat on vide their Order No. L - 300122188 -DJII ( B ) , 
dated , the 4th April, 1989 .

cess Operator is jus : fed and so the workman is not entitled

? contrary to tie provision of Clause 19 (c ) of the Certified

It will thus appear from the Standing Orders Clausc 19 
that the concerned workman has to give School Lcuving

unable to produce any documentary cvidence of his age, 
he sho ]l : tatu his age and mako a written declaration that 
the age stated by him is correct and thereafter the employee

M - 2 as I have already stated , is dated prior to the filling 
of the Attestation Form Evt. ML- 1 and was granted only 
10 days after the appointment of the concerned workman 
in Barauni Reſincry . It will also appear from Ext. M - 2 
that this is not a certificate regarding the assessment of 
age of the concerned workmun arrived at on scientific data . 
It is stated in Ext. M - 2 that the age according to the con 
cerned workman s own statement was 32 years and by this 
appearance liis age was 35 years. The age by appearance 
stated as 35 years by the Medical Oncer itself shows that 
he had stated the age only on the outward appearance of 
the concerned workman and that the doctor had not exa 
mined him to assess his age on any scientific method . Copsi 
der ng all these it will appear that Ext. M - 2 is not a 
doctor s certificate assessing the age of the concerned 
workman . 

It is stated by the workman that Ext. M - 2 is a Medcal 
Certificate about the fitness of the concerned workman for 
his cmployment in the Company s service and for that pur 
pose the doctor had examince the concerned workman . It 
is stated in Ext. M -2 that the doctor had examined the con 
cerned workman and " coulj not discover that he had any 
disease communicable otherwise constitutional affection or 
hodily infirmity which lie does not consider a disqualifica 
tion for employment in the nihco of Indian O ] Corporation ." 
It is clear therefore that Ext. 1 - 2 in only a fitness certificato 
given by the doctor to show that the concerned workman 
does not suffer from any defect so as to qualify him 
from his appointment. On perial of Ext, M -2 is apparent 
that this is a fitness and not a medical certificate assessing the 
age of the concerned workman . As held above Ext. M - 2 
cannot be the basis of age date of hirth of the concerned 
workman which is required to be recorded under clause 19 
of the Standing Orders of the Company . Ext. M - 2 was 
merely a fitness certificntc showing the age of the concerned 
workman as 35 years on appearance .

Finding of the enquiry officer of this charge is in accor

Section 18 ( xxiii) of the certified stanü ng order of the Cor 
poration says that negligence in discharge of duties in a 
misconduct. Again Section 19 (V ) of the said order says

According to him , he finding of the enqu ; y officer te iig

Now the question is whether the punishment awarded 
against the workman was in accordance with the gravity of 
thie offence committed by workunan K . D . M . Ali. In this 
respect contention of the management is that workman Ali 
is habitually negligent in his dutics and was warned on many 
other occasions. 


Circumstances prevailing at the reicvant time and nature 
of offence committed by the workman on 1 - 1 - 86 I am of 
opinion dismissal from serv ce is soinewhat excesive punish 
ment. Considering the entire circumstances of the case 
and age of the workman who is around 45 years as reported 
and not likely to get any employment in any other establish 
ment, I find lesser punishment than dismissal will meet the 
end of justice in this case . As a result it is held that the 
dismissal of the workman from service is to be replaced by 
another punishment of lesser kind. In the present circuns 
stances and having 00 scope for future employment in iny 
opinion workman if debarred from 4 annuai increments and 
stoppage of proinotion for 4 years will met the ends of 
justice . 


Management witness Lee Bee Sch stated that enquiry 
cilicer » . Choudhury long ago retired from service and at 
present lie is connded to wheel clair . He tuillier siated 

. Chouunury is not in a position to move about cr altend 
Court. Other witness ) . Pardhi stated that he was the 
presenting oilicer duly appointed by departmental auiliority . 
5 . Choudhury was the Enquiry Oliicei . Workman was 
present at the time of enquiry with lus Co -Worker who 
Cross examined witnesses of management side . He iwther 
stated Enquiry Ofhcer gave wil oppurtunities in the workman 
to defend himself. According io hun , workman filed a

Ex. 6 is the said writen :: rgument. Ex. 6 ( 1 ) is the signatuie 
of N . K . Sarmah co worker . Accordwg to him Ex. 7 is the

by his order Ex . 40 rejected the said appeal. Froin rne 
record of the enquiry proceed .ng I find enquiry ofiicer has

himself . Counsel for the workman argued that copy of 
the first report aleelly filed by employees of machanical 
Deptt. was not furnished to the workman so he was not in 
a position to know all about the charges levelled against

he filed his explanation . Ex. 3 accordingly . This explana 
tion Ex. 3 clearly proves that work man knew about all the 
charges levelled against hint before fiiling his explanation . 
Counsel for workman jurther contended that management

From Ex. 42 it is clear workman received copy of the enquiry 
report but he unfortunately lost it. Regarding examination

examined all witnesses at length . As such it appears that 
there was never any unfarness in the proceeding taken 
against the workman . Regarding the proving of charges ,

hm to prepare fresh snacks, which he did . 


In the result an award is hereby passed by substituting 
the punishment with stoppage of 4 annual increments and 
promotion for 4 years or the workrxan as indicated above . 
Workman is reinstated in his service with immed ate effect 
in his original grade with all back wages ninus 4 annual 
increments with immediate effect and promotion to next 
higher grade for 4 years from the date of his reinstatement . 
ment.

9 . On a perusal of the evidence adduced by both 
parties both oral and documentary , I can have no 
hesitation to hold that the second party Sri Mohanty 
declared his age to be 30 years as on 23 - 10 - 1961 
which was mentioned in his employment application 
form Ext. D and in the appointment letter Ext. E and 
on the basis thereof he was retired with effect from 
1 - 7 -86 as per the practice prevailing in the First 
Party - company . The evidence also clearly establish 
that the school leaving certificate Ext. A which was 
producent by the second party -workman before the 
Management of the company on the verge of his re 
tirement was not a genuine certificate , His contradic 
torv versions relating to the time of production of the

inference in the counter he stated that at the time of 
his initial annointmment in the company he produrer 
his school leaving certificate which he had obtained 
on 15 - 7 - 1960 . In his evidence he stated that he did 
not nroduce the school leaving certifirate at the time 
of his initial annointment but he produced the came 
sometime in 1980 . Such contradiction considered 
alonotvith the evidence of the Mannoement o witnesses 
clearly estahlish that by the time of his initial annnint. 
ment he did not possess the school leaving certifinate 
on which now he relies upon . It is very difficult to be

SCHEDULE 
" Whether the action of the Management of 

Sonepur Bazari Open Cast Project of Ms. 
E . C . Ltd ., in stopping Sri Krishna Kora , 
Wagon Loader from his work on and from 
17 -12 -86 is justified ? If not, to what relief 
is the concerned workman cntitled ? " 


2 . The case of the concerned workman Sri Krishna 
Kora in brief is that he was a waron loader of 
Kumarkhela Onen Cast Project (KK. O . C . P .) of 
Sonents Area under Eastern Coalfields Ltd . From 
17- 12- 86 the management refused to allow him to 
attend his normal duties on the alleged ground that 
he had hnen transferred to another colliery , He was 
not served with any formal order of transfer. But he 
was not given any work from 17 - 12 - 86 . The action 
of the management was highly illegal. It was only to 
harass and victimise the poor workman who coincs 
from a backward community of the societv . 

The concerned workman raised a dispute hefore 
the AT 

Raniguni. The attemnts of concilia 
tion failed . Then the matter was sent to Ministry of 
Lahour and ultimatelor the Ministry of Thons has 
referred the matter to this Tribunal for adjudication , 

3. The management hus contcaded inter- alia that 
the dispute is purely an individual disputc and not on 
industrial dispute us defined in Sccion 2 (k ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947. The alleged stopnage 
of work does not tantamount to dismissal or termina 
tion . So the Reference is bad in law . 

It is true that the concerned workinan Sri Krishna 
Kora was working as wagon loader of Kumarkhela 
Open Cast Project under Sonopur Area . This work 
man along with 17 other wagon loaders had applied 
for their transfer to Belbaid Colliery of Kunustoria 
Area to work as underground loader . A joint appli 
cation was filed by them before the authority and the 
same was forwarded to Cy. Chief Personnel Mana 
ger , Sanctoria hy a letter dated 8 - 12 - 86 (Ext. M - 1 ) . 
On receipt of the said application the said workmen 
including Krishna Kora were transferred to Belbaid 
Colliery by a letter dated 17119 - 12 - 86 ( Ext. M - 71 
as underpround loader and instruction was issued to 
relarse them by a letter dated 2431 -12 - 86 ( Ext . 
M - ) In pursuance of that instruction Sri Krishna 
Koro and Othere were released hy an Order dotrd 
1 - 1 -87 ( Ex. M -5 ) and L .P . C . was also issued dated 
6130 _ 1 87 ( Ext. M -4 ) . The story of stoppage of 
work is ahonntelv false . The workman has no foot 
ing and his claim is not maintainable , . 


" 9 - A . Notice of change - No employer, who pro 
poses to effect any change in the conditions of ser 
vice applicable to any workmen in respect of any 
matter specified in the Fourth Schedule , shall effect 
such change -- 
(a ) without giving to the workmen likely to be 

affected by such change a notice in the 
prescribed manner of the nature of the 

change proposed to be effected ; or 
(b ) within twenty - one days of giving such 

notice : 
Provided that no notice shall be required for 

cffecting any such change - 
(a ) where the change is cffected in pursuance 

of any [settlement or award ); or 
( b ) where the workmen likely to be affected 

by the change are persons to whom the 
Fundamental and Supplementary Rules, 
Civil Services (Classification , Control and 
Appeal) Rules, Civil Services ( Tem 
porary Servic : ) Rules, Revised Leave 
Rules, Civil Service Regulations, Civiu 
lians in Defonce Services, (Classification 
Control and Appeal) Rulcs for the Indian 
Railway Establishment Code or any 
other rules or regulations that may be 
notified in this behalf by the appropriate 

Government in the Official Gazette , apply . 
On the face of the documents filed by the manage 
ment, it appears that it is a clear case of transfer , hort 
the workman did not join his new place of work in 
spite of that order. So even by any stretch of imagi 
nation it cannot be said that it is a case of a discharge, 
clismissal, retrenchment nr termination to bring the 
case US 2 - A of the Industrial Disputes Act. 1947 , 
Moreover , from the matcrials on record I find that 
story of stoppage is a nvth . He was transferred but 
he did not join his duty . With due respect to the con 
tention of the learned Lawyer for the management 
I like to say that it is ? Court of social justce . The 
management has not stated anywhere that even now 
the management is ready to allow him to join the 
duty though there was no order of stoppage . 


5 . From the statenient of the workman himself as 
WW - 1 I find that he never went to Bohaid rollierv 
to work there As a cumulative result it has become a 
case of stonnage and considering the princinles of 
natural iustice I find that it mav he considered as a 
caca Uls 2 - A of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947. 
Folinwing the nrincinles of natural instire ant for 
elds of justice I find that the Reference is not bad .

5 . In this connection , the learned Advocac for 
the Workman also placed reliance on RAJ NDER 
KUMAR KINDRA V. DELHI ADMINISTRATION 
THROUGH SECRETARY (LABOUR ) AND 
OTHERS [ 1984 (II) LLJ, page 517 ) a judgement 
of the Supreme Court. While considering the ques 
tida of back wages , the Supreme Court held that 
when the worker therein stated that during his 
forced absence from employment since the date of 
tremination of his service, he was maintaining his 
family by helping the father - in -law Tara Chand 
who owns coal depots and that he and the mem 
bers of his family lived with his father- in -law and 
that he had no alternative source of maintenance . 
It was held that it cannot be considered as gainful 
employment and in that connection the Supreme 
Court observed as follows : 


2 . l hereupon ihe workman filed W . P . No. 9608 
of you and the High Court remanded the matter 
by viriuc ot its Order dt. 27- 0 - 1967. It was re 
manuud with the following observations " The Lab 
our court shall now reconsider the aspect with respect 
to the relevant material before it , whether the 
workinın is entitled for back wages or not and if 
entitled , in what measure and the Writ Petition is 
allowed to thic cxtent indicated above . Hence the 
mat. er is taken up for consideration by this Tribu 
nal. After thc mater was remanded the workman 
was recalled and he examined himself and admited 
that he worked from 26 - 7 - 1979 onwards till 
28 - 9 - 1982 ai Bailadilla Iron Ore Projcot, Bacheli 
as Mechanic -I and from that date he was suspen 
ded . He also admitted that he received the amount 
mentioned in Ext . M 4 . In cross examination , he 
admitted that he did not file pay sheet of Bailadilla 
Project and did not file any papers of the domestic 
ciquiry conducted after his suspension and he 
denied the suggestion that he is still working there . 
He also admitted that he did not file any docu 
ments from the Revenue Department to show that 
he has no land and cultivating others land as a 
tenant, his educational qualification is 8th Class. He 
also denied the suggestion that in 1982 when his 
rights to join in the Respondent Company accrued , 
as he was gainfully employed and was not inter 
csted , his right of employment was forftited . 

3 . Thus evidently the entire matter rests on Ex. 
M 4 . Ex, M 4 is a letter from the Personnal Man 
ager, N . D . M . C . wherein it was stated that " the 
worker in question is working as Mechanic I in 
this Project w . e . f . 26 - 7 - 1979. A statement show 
ing his nionthwise salary is enclosed herewith fur 
your information " . The statement ran from 
26 - 7 - 1979 to 28 - 9 - 1982 only . It was mentioned 
therein that from the date only subsistence allow 
0 .1ce was being paid as he was under suspension . 
This letter was dt. 2 - 5 - 1983 and we are in 1990 . 
If really the Management is interested or N .M . D . C . 
is interested , they must come forward with full 
picture showing wheher his service were terminated 
at Bachali. the place of his work and if so upto 
what date and what happened after he was SUIS -- 
pended Hc was examined in this Tribunal on 
13- 7 - 199. Of course suggestion were made that he 
is still working there ctc . No doubt the Manage 
ment that produced Ex , M 4 will be definitely in a 
better position to come uut with the full particulars 
of the concerned workman . Simply on the basis of 
a suggestion that as he did not file document from 
the Revenuç Department to show that he got no 
land of his own . It cannot be concluded that he 
was cultivating lands of others. 

4 . The question to be decided now is only with 
regard to back wage . In these circumstances, this 
Tribunal holds that the Petitioner is entitled for 
hack wages for the said period , the amount which 
he sought to get from Singareni Collieries froni 
26 - 7 - 1879 to October, 1982 minus the gross 
amount mentioned in column 2 of Ex. M 4 . The 
contention that he is not entitled for any thing, 
agrued the argument that the petitioncr -workman is 
mo ? entitled o any sum as he was gainfully emp 
loyed somewhere during he relevant period , cannot 
he cntertained at all , 

3775 01/90 - 16 


" If this is gainful employment, the employer 

can contend that the dismissed employee 
in order to keep his body and soul to 
gether, had taken to begging and that 
would as well be a gainful employment . 
The gross perversity with which the emn. 
loyer had approached this case has left 
us stunned . If the employer, after an 
utterly unsustainable termination order of 
service wants to deny back wages on the 
ground that the appellant and the mem 
bers of his familv were staving with the 
father- in -law of the anpellant three 
was no alternative source of maint- monnan 
and during this perind anpellant was heln . 
ing his father -in -law Tara Chand who 
had a coal depot, it cannot be said that 
the appellant was gainfully employed . 
This was the only evidence in support of 
the submission that during his forced 
absence from service he was gainfully em 
ploved . This cannot be said to ha cainful 
employment so as to reject the claim for 
hack wages. There is no evidence on the 
record to show that the appellant was min . 
fully emploved during the neriod of his 
absence from service. Therefore , the an 
pellant would be entitled to full hack 
wages and all oonsequential benefits ." 


6 . Nertly reliance was placed on S . G . CHEMI 
CALS AND DYES TRADING EMPLOYEES 
UNION and S . G . CHEMICALS AND DYES 
TRADING LIMITED AND ANOTHER ( 1986 (I ) 
LLJ page 490 ) Another judgement of the Supreme 
Court. Here also a contenion was raised by the 
Management that after closure of the establishment, 
the employees must have taken up alternative emp 
loyment and hence an enquiry should be direc ed 
to be made into the amount received by them - from 
such alternative employment so as tosct off the 
amounts , so received against the backwages and 
future salary payable to them etc . But the Supreme 
Court refused and said it is possible that rather than 
starve while awaiting the final decision . some of 
the workman might have taken to alternative emo 
Tronment and there would be no difficulty in adjust 
ing the back wages against the amount of retrench 
mçnt compensation etc.,

tioned in column 2 of Ex . M 4 .

द्वारा उक्त विवाद को स्यायनिर्णयन के लिए उक्त राष्ट्रीय प्रौद्योगिक

15 , I would like to refer to what was held in the 
case of Shri kurac Jha Versus Bihar State Electricity 
Board 1990 Lab IC NOC 97 Fatna . It Was held that 
an adverse entry in the confidential character roll 
cannot be ac ed upon to deny Promotional opportu 
nity wiles ; it is communicated to the person concern 
ed so that he has an opportunity to improve his work 
and conduct or to explain the circumstances leading 
to the report. It was furthter held that it is true that 
the adverse entries in confidential character roll was 
communicated to the cmplovee prior to the passing 
of the order denying him promotion but the authori 
tics did not consider the representation made by him . 
____ 16. The question which now arises whether the 
Tribunal should take upon itself thc Roll of D .P . C . 
or it should leave to the D . P . C . Shri V . K . Gupta , 
authorised presentative for the management has 
argued that Tribunal camot take upon itself the roll 
of the D . P . C . It should be left to the D . P . C to consi 
der his case . 

17 . I naic heard Shri Nair also on this point and 
considering the ruling cited abovc by me, I am of 
the opinion , that suítulle direction should be issued 
in the present case to the management to refer the 
matter of the workman s promotion for considera 
tion to D .I . C and if the D .P .C ignoring the adverse 
entry of punishment finds liim suitable for proinution 
to the post of Store Kecper then the management 
should place him in the list of promotecs of 1984 .

Bina Project of Northern Coalfields ) uilcd in deny 
ing promotio ı tc the workman w . c.f. 13 - 11-84 is 
neither leg i nor justified . As observed above the 
management is directed to refer the matter of promo 
tion of the workman from the post of Assistant Store 
Kecper to the post of Store Keeper to the DPC with 
the request to consider the workman s casc afresh in 
the light of thc observations made by me above , i. e . 
without taking into corsideration the adverse ( ntry 
regarding the punishment awarded to him and if the 
D . P . C . finds him fit and suitable for promotion to 
the post of Store Keeper , to place him in the list of 
candidates for promotion to the post of core keeper 
prepared in 1984 .

BEFORE THE CENTRAL GOVERNMENT 
INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL NO . 1 , DHANBAD . 


In the matter of a reference under section 10 (1 ) ( d ) 

of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 


Reference No. 73 of 1983 . 


E . C . Lid ., Ms. V . C . Ltd . and Ms. N . E . C . Liu . 
for administrasive convenience and oulier reasons. 
The present dispute relates to the coul nines owned 
and managed by Vis. Tata vion & Sicei company 
Lid ., such as, Janiududa , Digwadih , û & 7 Pilis 
jamadoba Corbery , Sijua- bhe atand and Ivaikei 
Lollicries inciuding Power House . In those collie 
nies workmen who operate , start, stop , clean and 
Beriorm all sinds vi duties and iesponsibilities while 
11 e machineries arv in mololl, have been wrongly 
designated as khalasis , such as, Puinp Khalasi , Faui 
Slidiasi , Haulage Koalası , Compressor Kiai , 
Winding Engine Khalası etc . in accordance with 
different jobs performed by wein , and thus derogatory 
designation has been continung for the last 20 to 
30 years. The word "knalasi is not a suitable 
fomenciature for those workmen who operate dille 
rent types of machines. Derogatory designation like 
coolie and kha :üsi were invented and iinpu . ed on 
workmen for fiumiliating them by Britisi Rulers . 
After independence , revisions have been made iro . 
ime to time and the designation of coolie has been 
Changed to iviazdoors , but no revisiou has been made 
in the noinenciature of khalasi due to oversight and 
lack of proper representation at the time when wages 
were revised and redesignations wade. The tcini 

Khalasi originates from the word "Khalas which 
means removal, release or act of ousting or clearing 
goods and thus *Khalasi is a person who is engaged 
in removing, releasing or clearing goods or maicials 
from any estabiishment or working place . Thus, the 
designation of Khalasi is inconsistent with the nature 
of job performed by the workmen designated as

coinpetent to consider this demand . In my view , 
the contention of Shri S , S , Mukherjee is not sustain 
able becauso of the fact that the demand of the union 
ay expressed in the terms of reference should not be 
read in isolation but in its entirety . In the present 
case the demand of the unioni , as envisaged in 1lje 
terms of reference, comprises of two parts - (i) for 
change of designation from khalasi to operator and 
( ii ) for upgradation . Upon consideration of the 
demand of the union in entirety, I am constrained 
to hold that it is essentially an industrial dispute 
Wljich can be adjudicated upon hy this Tribunal.

7 . Admittedly , the management of Ms. TISCO 
has got five collicries in tứe district of Dhanbad , 
namely , Jamadoba , Digwallili, 6 & 7 Pits Jamadoba 
Colliery , Sijua-Bholetand and Malkera Colliery . 
Undisputedly the management of Ms. TISCO collie 
ries has accepted and adopted the recommendations 
for Central Wage Board for Coal Mining Industry 
(hereinafter referred to as Central Wage Board 
Recomincndations) and National Coal Wage Agree 
ment (hereinafter refcrred to as N . C . W . A . for bre 
vity ) . 

8 . Shri S . S . Mukherjee , Advocate for the manage 
ment, has contended that the sponsoring union , Santa 
Mazdoor Sangh , has no locus standito raise the 
industrial dispute for the general body of workmcn . 
He has submitted that Janta Mazdoor Sangh is neither 
the recognised nor the representative union operating 
in Ms. TISCO collieries , 

9 . Shri B . N . Sharma , Joint General Secretary of 
the sponsoring union , has contended that the union 
was authorised by general body of workmen to raiso 
the instant industrial dispute and according to him 
it is not necessary that an industrial dispute should 
invariably raised by a recognised union . He has 
submitted further that all that is required is that the 
union must be a representative body of a substantial 
number of workmen , 

10 . The management has not adduced any evidence 
in support of its contention that Janta Mazdoor Sangh 
has no locus standi to raise the present industrial 
dispute . On the other hand, the union has placed 
on record its letter dated 25 -5 -83 (Ext. W - 4 ) addres 
sed to the Asstt . Labour Commissioner (Central) , 
Dhanbad - II whcrely it has been asserted that the 
dispute was raised as workmen had passed a reso 
lution authorising Santa Mazdoor Sangh to sponsor 
their dispute and to represent them before A . L . C . 
( C ) . The witness for the management has not dis 
puted or denied the correctness of statement milde 
in this letter . This being the evidence , I am cons 
trained to hold that the concerned workmen autho 
rised Janta Mazdoor Sangh to raise the present 
industrial dispute. 

It is not necessary that the recognised unions only 
are competent to raise an industrial dispute . Any 
union having a following of substantial workmen can 
raise an industrial dispute . In the present case since 
Janta Mazdoor Sangh although not a recognised 
union , was authorised by the concerned workman to 
raise the present industrial dispute , I am constrained 
to hold that the present industrial dispute raised by 
this union is sustainable . 

11. Then again , Shri S . S . Mukherjec has further 
contended that the demand of the union for change 
of noinenclature from Khalasis to Operators is int 
an industrial dispute and hence this Tribunnt is 17 


@ alio 


12 . In canvassing its demand for change of nomen 
clature the union has essentially underscored two 
points - - (i ) that the designation of khalasi is inconsis 
tant with the nature of job performed by khalasis , 
such as pump khalasi, fan khalasi, haulage khalasi, 
compressor khulasi , winding cngine khalasi and 
( ii) that designation of khalasi is a stamp of humilia 
tion having its legacy from former British Rulers. 

The management has contested the claim for change 
of designation on the plea that designation of khalasi 
has got the sanction of Central Wage Board Recom 
mendation and any change will have widespread 
repercussion concerning other categories of workmen 
covered by the said recommendations. The manage 
ment has not spelt out in specific turn either in its 
pleading or in evidence the widespreadı repercussions 
concerning other categories of workmen that are 
likely to flow if such chance of designation is effected . 
Anyway, this dispute does not remain a live issue 
any longer. It appears that Mſs. B . C . C . Ltd , already 
cffected the change of designation from khalasi to 
oneratorslattendants way back in October, 1980 
(Ext. W - 2 ) . The management of M /s. TISCO 
collieries does not seem to be oblivion to this inas 
much as it hud effected such change of designation 
Trom khalasi to operators with effect from 10 - 1 - 85 
alongwith introduction of cadre scheme for khalasis 
operators . MW - 1 Şri Bidyut Ghosh was posted tu 
Sijua colliery , Malkera colliery and Samadoha colliery 
as Personnel Officer in the course of his service ; now 
he is posted in the Central Administrative Office at 
Jamadoba. He has stated emphatically that the 
desionation of workmen who were carlier designated 

khalasis has since heen changed as operators and 
that this change of designation from khalasi to opera 
lur was done as per cadre scheme of the company 
which has been marked Ext . M . 4 and that the cadre 
schems came into force with effect from 10 - 1 - 1985 . 
Thus, it is seen that the demand of the union for 
change of designation from khalasi to operator has 
ultimately heen accepted hy the management and in 
the circumstances it is not necessary to delve dce ; 
into the matter any more . 

13 . Now , we are left with the other part of the 
demand of the union for upgradation of the work 
men to one entegory higher than their existing cate 
gory. The reison d etre provided by the union for 
ungradation of the workmen , earlier callcd khalasi 
1709 desimated a operator, is their stapnation in the 
same category and scale of pay for a long span of 
time spreading over 20 to 30 years

laid down promotion policy for such category of 
workmen . In suppoit of his contention he referred 
me to the letter dated 4 6 - 10 - 80 addressed by General 
Manager (Per:none!) to all General Managers , Chief 
Engineers ( E & M ) , Chief Mining Engineer ( C ) and 
all Personnel Managers (Ext . W - 2 ) . But this letter 
does not clarify what was the cadre scheme formulated 
and promotion policy adopted by J. B . C . C .I . Any 
way, it must be borne in mind that the workmen 
joined the service of the employer not only to cke 
out their living but also for, bettering their standard 
of living. That apart, if they are left to stagnate in 
the same post crer the years, it may lead to frustra 
tion and despondency amongst them which may 
effect their cfficiency in the long run . In this view 
of the matter, every employer , enlightened or not 
should endeavour to offer some prospect in service 
career by evolving a workable cadre scheme and pro 
motion policy . Nevertheless , the fact cannot be 
ignored that in doing so the financial constraint of 
the establishment should be taken into consideration . 
In the present case the union has laid no evidence 
to show that the moonromant nf M /s. TISCO has 
got sufficient : i to effectuate a work 
able cadre scheme and promotion policy. However , 
it is in evidence that the management has introduced 
cadre scheme (Est . M - 4 ) for the workmen now 
designated as operators with promotion policy . There 
is no knowing of the position whether the union is 
satisfied with the cadre scheme and promotion policy 
as framoil by the management. It has not been firmly 
established by cvidence that even after introduction 
of such cadre scheme any workman has been stag 
nating in the same categorV . On the contrary WW - 2 
Nepal.Mahato has stated that he has been working 
as Haulage Operator in Siiua colliery for the last 
12 years in a permanent post and earlier he was 
placed in Category - III and on 10 - 1 - 85 he was desig 
nated as onerator and placed in Category - IV . Even 
so , there may be cases where stagnation in the same 
nost is the order of the day. For such case : N . C . W . A . 
IV to which the management of Ms. TISCO colliery 
is a party fox spelt out certain provisions by agree 
ment. Pity 2 . 11 of the Agreement reads as follows : 
“ 2 . 11. Upgradation of Daily Rated & Montlly 

Rated emnloyees who have remained in the 

same categorylgrade for 10 years or more : 
Daily rated and monthly rated employees who 

have remained in the same categorylgrade 
for a period of 10 years or more would 
he upgraded to the next higher category 
grade and such yrgradation will. take 
effect from 1st July 1989. Emplovees 
completing 10 years of service in the same 
categorylgrade subsequently will be lin 
graded with effect from 1st July , 1990 
and 1st July , 1991 respectively . How 
ever , cuch upgraded emnloyees will 
continue to do the existing jobs.”

मई दिल्लं । 10 दिसम्बर, 1990 
फा . प्रा . 3455 यत: मैसर्स पेट्रोफिल्स फोप्रापरेटिव लिमिटेड पोस्ट 
प्राफिस पेट्रोमिफल्स जिला बड़ीवा तथा समस्त भारत स्थित इसकी 
शाखाएं ( उसके प्रागे यहाँ जहाँ कहीं भी उक्त स्थापना शब्द का प्रयोग 
हो इससे मभिप्राय उक्त स्थापना से है ) ने कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि 
पौर प्रकीर्ण उपबंध अधिनियम 1952 ( 1952 का 19 ) इसके 
मागे उक्त अधिनियम के नाम से निर्दिष्ट की धारा 17 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) के खर ( क ) के अन्तर्गत छूट प्राप्त करने के लिये प्रादेवन 
किया है । 

यह केन्द्र सरकार की राय में उस स्थापना के कर्मचारियों के लिये 
तैयार किये गये भविष्य निधि नियमों में प्रभावान की दर उक्त 
अधिनियम की धारा 8 में उल्लिखित फर्मचारी प्रशवान की दर से कम 
नहीं है तथा इसके कर्मचारियों को मिलने वाले भविष्य निधि लाभ 
सन्त अधिनियम तथा कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि स्कीम 1952 ( इसके 
मागे जहाँ कहीं भी स्कीम शब्द का प्रयोग किया गया है उससे 
अभिप्रायं उक्त स्कीम से है ) में उल्लिखित लाभों से किसी भी प्रकार 
से कम नहीं है जो इस वर्ग की स्थापमानों में कार्यरत कर्मचारियों 
को उपलब्ध है । 

अब इसलिये उपस प्रधिनियम की धारा 17 की उपधारा एक के 
बंड ( क ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और संलग्न 
प्रमुसूची में वर्णित शर्तों के अधीन केन्द्रीय सरकार इसके द्वारा उक्त 
स्थापना को उपस स्कीम के सभी उपबन्धों के लागू होने से 1- 12-1987 
से छूट प्रदान करती है । 


5. यदि स्यापना को छूट न दी जाती तो थे सभी कर्मचारी (जैसे 
उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 2 ( ब ) में निश्चित किया मया है ) जो सबस्य 
मनने के पात्र होते सवस्प बनाये जायेंगे । 
____ 6. जहां एक कर्मचारी जो कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि ( कानूनी ) 
या किसी अन्य छूट प्राप्त स्थापना का पहले से सदस्य है को अपनी 
स्थापना में काम पर लगाया जाता है तो नियोक्ता उसे निधि का 
तुरन्त सदस्य बनायेगा और ऐसे कर्मचारी के पिछले नियोक्ता के पास 
भविष्य निधि लेखे में संचयों को अंतरित कराने और उसके लेखे में 
जमा कराने की व्यवस्था करेगा । 
___ 7. केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि प्रायुक्त के द्वारा अथवा केन्द्रीय सरकार 
को धारा जैसे भी मामला हो समय समय पर दिये गये निवेशों के 
अनुसार भविष्य निधि के प्रबन्ध के लिये नियोक्ता म्यासी बोर्ड की 
स्थापना करेगा । . 

8. भविष्य निधि न्यासी मोर्स में निहित होगा जो मन्य बातों 
के होते हुए भविष्य निधि में प्राय के उचित लेखों और भविष्य 
निधि से प्रदायगियों और उनकी अभिरक्षा में शेषों के लिये फर्मचारी 
भविष्य निधि संगठन के उत्तरवायी होगा । 

9. न्यासी मोठं कम से कम 3 माह में एक बार बैठक करगे मौर 
के सरकार द्वारा समय समय पर जारी किये गये मार्ग निदेशों के 
प्रभुसार कार्य करेंगें । केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि प्रायुक्त को मधि कार 
होगा कि यह किसी अन्य योग्य लेखा परीक्षक से खातों को दुबारा 
लेखा पक्षा कराये और ऐसे पुनः लेखा-परीक्षा के खर्च नियोक्ता 
बहन रेगा । 
____ 10. न्यासी बोर्ड द्वारा रखे गये भविष्य निधि खे अर्हता प्रसुप्त 
मिष्पक्ष भार्टर्स अकाउन्टेन्ट द्वार। वार्षिक लेखा परीक्षा के प्रध्ययीन 
होंगे । जहाँ मायश्यक समझा जाये केन्द्रीय भषिष्य निधि प्रायुक्त को 
किमी प्रन्य अर्हता प्राप्त लेखा-परीक्षक धारा लेनों को पुनः परीक्षा 
कराने का अधिकार होगा और इस पर हुमा व्यय नियोक्ता द्वारा 
वहन किया जायेगा । 


अनुसूची 


11. प्रत्येक वर्ष स्थापना के लेखा परीक्षित तुलन पत्र के साथ 
लेखा परीक्षित वार्षिक भविष्य निधि लेखों की एक प्रति वित्तीय वर्ष 
की समाप्ति के छः के अन्दर क्षेत्रीय भविष्य निधि भायुक्त को 
प्रस्तुत की जायेगी । इस प्रयोजन के लिये भविष्य निधि का वित्तीय 
वर्ष पहली अप्रैल से 31 मार्च तक होगा । 
____ 12. नियोक्ता प्रतिमाह भविष्य निधि के देय अपने कर्मचारियों 
के भगवानों की प्रागागी माह की 15 तारीख तक न्यासी बोर्ड को 
अन्तरित कर देगा । मंशवानों की विलम्ब से प्रदायगी करने के लिये 
समान परिस्थितियों में नियोक्ता नुकसानी देने का उसी प्रकार उत्तर 
दायी होगा जिस प्रकार एक न छूट प्राप्त स्थापना उत्तरदायी होती है । 


1. उक्त स्थापमा से संबंधित नियोक्ता केन्द्र सरकार के द्वारा समय 
समय पर दिये गये निदेश के अनुसार उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 
की उपधारा ( 3 ) के खंड ( क ) में उल्लिखित निरीक्षण के लिये 
सुविधाएं प्रदान करेगा और ऐसे निरीक्षण प्रभार की अदायगी प्रत्येक 
माह की समाप्ति के 15 दिन के अन्दर करेगा । 

2. छन- छूट प्राप्त स्थापनामों के संबध में उम्त अधिनियम मौर 
उसके अधीन सृजित उक्त स्कीम के अन्तर्गत देय प्रशदान के दर से 
सपना के भविष्य निधि नियमों के प्रस्तर्गत वेय मंशवान का पर 
किसी समय भी कम न होगा । 

3. पेशगियों के मामले में छूट प्राप्त स्थापना की स्कीम कर्मचारी 
भविष्य निधि स्कीम 1952 से कम हितकर नहीं होगी । 

4. उक्त स्कीम में कोई भी संशोधन जो स्थापना के वर्तमान 
नियमों से अधिक लाभकारी है उम पर अपने पाप लागू किया जायेगा । 
उमा स्थापना के भविष्य निधि नियमों में कोई भी संशोधन क्षेत्रीय 
भविष्य निधि पायुक्त की पूर्व मनुमति के बगैर नहीं किया जायेगा 
और वहां किसी संशोधन से उक्त स्थापना के कर्मचारियों के हित के 
प्रतिकूल प्रभावी होने की सम्भावना है वहां अपनी पनुमति देने से 


13 न्यासी बोर्ड सरकार द्वारा समय समय दिये गये निदेशों के 
अनुसार निधि में जमा राशियों का निवेश करेगा । प्रतिभूतियां न्यासी 
धो के नाम पर प्राप्त की जायेंगी और भारतीय रिजर्व बैंक के जमा 
नियंरण में अनुसूचित बैंक की अभिरक्षा में रखा जायेगा । 
___ 14. सरकार के निदेशों के अनुसार निदेश न करने पर न्यासी 
घोई मलग -अलग रूप से भौर एक साथ केन्द्रीय भविष्य निधि , मायुक्त 
या उसके प्रतिनिधियों द्वारा लगाये गये अधिक प्रभार का उत्तरषायी 
होगा । 


____ 15. ग्यासी बोडें एक वस्तु ब्यौरा रजिस्टर सैयार करेगा और 
ग्याज पौर विमोचन माय को समय पर वसूली स.मिश्चित करेगा ।

अपेक्षित है कि मगनेसाइट खनन उद्योग , जोकि औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 
1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) को प्रथम अनमूची में प्रविष्टि 24मारा शामिल 
है , को उक्त अधिनियम के प्रयोजनों के लिए लोक उपयोगी सेवा घोषित 
किया जाना चाहिए , 

अतः प्रम , मायोगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
की धारा 2 के लंड ( ) के उपखंर ( vi ) द्वारा प्रसस शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त उद्योग को उक्त अधिनियम के प्रयोजनों 
के लिए नफाल प्रभाव से छह मास को मालावधि के लिए लोक उपयोगी 
सेवा शोपित करती है ।

New Delhi , the 11th December, 1990 


S. O . 3459.- --In pursuance of Section 17 of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947), the 
Central Government hereby publishes the award of 
the Industrial Tribunal, Ahmedabad as shown in the 
Annexure in the industrial dispute between the emp 
loyers in relation to the Bank of Baroda and their 
workmen , which was received by the Central Gov 
crnment. 


ANNEXURE 


New Delhi, the 3rd December, 1990 
S . O . 3457. -- Whereas the Central Government is 
satisfied that the public interest requires that the 
Magnesito Mining Industry , which is covered by entry 
24 in the First Schedule to the Industrial Disputes 

Act, 1947 (14 of 1947) , should be declared to be a 
public utility service for the purposes of the said 
Act . 


BEFORE SHRI N. A . CHAUHAN. INDUSTRIAL 
TRIBUNAL , CENTRAL, AHMEDABAD 

Reference (ITC ) No . 57| 89 
Bank of Baroda , Vadodara 

.. .1st party 

Vs. 
Its workmen 

. .. 2nd party 
Whether the action of the Bank in terminating 

the services of its employee Shri D . N . 

Varniamiya is proper or not. 
Advocate of the 1st party : Shri K . V . Shah 
Advocate for the 2nd party : H . U . Parmar . 


Now , therefore , in cxercise of the powers confer 
red by sub- clause ( vi ) of clause ( n ) of section 2 of 
the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the 
Central Government hereby declares with immediate 
effect the said industry . to be a public utility service 
for the purposes of the said Act for a period of six 
months.

12 . The submission of Shri Shah is such that if the 
misconduct committed by the said employee is taken 
into consideration , the punishment of termination met 
ed out to him by the 1st party Bank cannot be termed 
as having been meted out with ulterior motive. There 
fore , intereference with the same by this Tribunal will 
not be termed as picper . In support of his submission 
Shri Shah has relied upon the judgment of the Supreme 
Court in the matter of Hindustan Steel Ltd ., Vs. A . K . 
Roy . which judgement has been published at page 
1165 of Lab . I . C . 1970 . The fact of the said matter 
was such that Mr. Roy was taken up as trade appren 
tice for 3 years by the owner establishment. At the 
end of the period of 3 months he was appointed as a 
skilled workman and as mentioned in the appointment 
letter , he had to give an undertaking to put in at least 
five years service . As per the practice of the owner 
establishment they were carrying out private investi 
gation of the person to whom they take up in service . 
Accordingly , private investigation of the said employee 
was carried out. And the report of the said investiga 
tion was to the eflect that the interest of the establish 
ment was to be endangered by continuing the said em 
ployee in the service because the said employee had 
taken part in an illegal strike and he was instigating 
the other employees to resort to illegal strike. On re 
ceipt of the report of this type, the owner establishment 
terminated Shri Roy from service by giving 3 months 
notice . Shri Roy challenged the said order . The Tri 
bunal did not accept the allegation that Roy was ter 
minated with ulterior motive by the owner establish 
ment; but had come to the conclusion that the 
said employee was not given an opportunity 
during which he was terminated . Therefore , the ac 
tion of termination cannot he termed legal. 
Therefore, order was passed in favour of Roy to re 
instate him with full back wages . The said order 
was challenged before the High Court of the State . 
The High Court also came to the similar conclusion 
like the Industrial Tribunal and the order of the 
Tribunal was not modified . Thereupon the owner 
establishment took the said chapter before the Sup 
reme Court . The Supreme Court by its judgement 
came to the conclusion that the action of tarminating 
the said employee cannot be said to have heen taken 
with ulterior motive : but the order of terminating him 
can be termed as illegal hecause the procedinos to 
be instituted before termination were not followed . 
But if the special facts of the chantar are taken into 
consideration the order of reinstating the said em 
ployee in service will not ha termed as oroner . There 
fore , it would be proper if he is oivan compensation 
instead of reinstating him . Therefore , order was 
passed to give compensation to the said einnlovee . 
This judgement can in no way becoma annlicable to 
the case before us. It is necessary to note that in our 
case there is no private report that the interest of the 
1st party Bank will be endangered by continuing the 
said employze in service . As stated above , the said 
3275 GT/ 90 - 20 . 


employee has stolen a fan and ordinary crockery . 
Therefore , it cannot be said that he will continue to 
steal permanently . Moreover , by continuing him in 
service , there is not likely to be any obstruction in 
the daily transactions of the Bank . Therefore , the 
finding of the said judgement cannot be applied to 
our case . Moreover, it is necessary to note here that 
at the time of making of this order by the Supreme 
Court, Section 11- K was not added to the Act of 
1947 and as stated above it is only iri the Section 
11 - K that the Tribunal is empowered with the autho 
rity to modify the punishment deemed improper by it. 
Therefore , if the Tribunal feels that the punishment 
meted out by the owner is not proper, the Tribunal 
has been empowered with additional authority to 
modify the decision of the owner. Therefore , the sub 
mission that the Tribunal is empowered to modify the 
decision of the owner under Section 11 - K only if the 
Tribunal feels that the decision of the owner was taken 
by ulterior motive , cannot be accepted for a moment. 

13 . Shri Shah relies upon a judgement in the matter 
of Air India Corporation Vs. Rebello , which is 
published on page 669 or Lab . I. C ., 1972 . In the 
said matter Rebello was terniinated , because he had 
lost confidence . The said order was challenged. In 
one of the reasons for losing the confidence , it was 
found that the said employee Rebello had such type 
of relations with an Air Hostess due to which the 
owner establishment may fejl ashamed . As per the 
private report it was established that the action of 
terminating Rebello was not taken with ulterior 
motive . In such circumstances the Supreme Court 
had delivered the judgement that the action of ter 
minating Rebello cannot be interefered with . The 
fact in our case is quite different. In our case the 
Bank does not say that the interest of the Bank will 
be endangered if the said employee is continued in 
service. Moreover , at the time when the Supreme 
Court delivered the said judgement , as stated above, 
Section 11- K was not in existence in the Act of 1947 . 
Therefore , the finding of that judgement cannot be 
applied to our case, because , after the addition of Sec 
tion 11 - K the Tribunal has been empowered with 
additional authority to modify the punishment. 

14 . Shri Shah relies on the Judgement of the Bom 
bay High Court in the matter of Sarabhai M . Chemi 
cals Ltd . Vs. M . S . Ajmera , which judgement was 
delivered on 12 - 7 - 1979. in the matter of Spl. Civil 
Application No. 1777 75 and whose zerox copy has 
been submitted at Annexure C along with the written 
arguments. In the said chapter , the employee as 
ignored the order of the owner and the said allegation 
was established : therefore , he was terminated from 
service . On behalf of the workman it was submitted 
that the same cannot be termed as misconduct if it is 
corriant ? ? * ,** the link Court of Bombay did 
no accept the said submission . Also the misconduct 
committed by the employee was obstructing mainte 
nance of discipline. As such though the employee 
committed the misconduct once but the eniployee was 
occupying such a position that if such type of em 
ployees commit such misconduct , the same would 
result in indiscipline amongst the other einployces. 
Therefore , although it was committed only once, the 
Bombay High Court has termed the said misconduct 
as fit for termination from service The finding of this 
judgement cannot be applied to our case , because due 
to the mis-conduct committed by the said enployce ,

New Delhi, the 11th December, 1990 
S. O . 3460 . — In exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
section ( 1 ) of section 10 of the Contract Labour ( Regula 
tion and Abolition) Act , 1970 ( 37 of 1970 ), the Central 
Government, after consultation with the Central Board , 
hereby prohibits the employment of contract labour in tho 
works /opcrations specified in thọ following Schedule, in tho 
coal washery in the country , with effect from the date of 
publication of this notification in the Official Gazetto . 

SCHEDULE 
(i) Transport of middling ; and 
( il) Removal of slurry . 

[ U . 23013/ 31/ 87-LW ] 
S . C . SHARMA, Under Secy . 


नई विरुली 11 दिसम्बर , 1990 
का . मा . 3480. – केन्द्रीय सरकार ठेका श्रम ( विनियमन पौर 
उत्पादन ) अधिनियम 1970 ( 1970 का 37 ) की धारा 10 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदस शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय बोर्ड 
से परामर्श करने के पश्चात् इस पधिसूचना के राजपत्र में प्रकाशम 
की तारीख से वेश में कोयला धोवनशाला में निम्नलिखित अनुसूची 
में विनिर्दिष्ट संकर्म संत्रियानों में ठेका श्रम प्रसिषित करती है ।
